# ERP — End-to-End UAT Results

**Run date:** 2026-06-21  
**Environment:** local durable stack — backend `http://localhost:8081` (dev profile), Postgres `:5434` (durable, not wiped), web `:4200`  
**Method:** live API end-to-end testing by an automated QA agent fleet (real HTTP calls) + Playwright UI smoke. Existing production-like data reused; new transactions layered on top.  
**Grading:** PASS = system behaved correctly (INCLUDING correct rejection of invalid input — e.g. wrong password returning 401 is a PASS). FAIL = behaviour diverged (wrong result, 500, missing validation/permission gate). BLOCKED = could not execute.  

## 1. Executive summary

| Metric | Count | % |
|---|---:|---:|
| Total tests | 2664 | 100% |
| PASS | 2569 | 96.4% |
| FAIL | 90 | 3.4% |
| BLOCKED | 5 | 0.2% |

**Defects by severity:** Critical: 8  ·  Medium: 28  ·  High: 27  ·  Low: 27

Agents reporting: 41

| DEF-002 | Critical | Accounts Payable | ApStatementController | AP sub-ledger vs GL reconciliation difference is non-zero (data integrity check) | `GET /api/v1/ap/statement/reconciliation` | difference should be 0 per BR-AP-02; GL 2100 credit balance should equal AP sub-ledger outstanding | HTTP 200; subLedgerTotal=940633.7, glControlBalance=-1045055.54, difference=1985689.24. GL 2100 trial-balance net=+1045055.54 (debit > credit, unusual for a liability), negated to -1045055.54. Difference is not 0. |

Query has no status filter. Actual result was 409. | HTTP 409; errors=[A tier with min_qty 1.00 already exists for this product+price_list.]; existsDuplicateBreak does not filter on status=ACTIVE so deactivated tiers permanently block their minQty slot |

GRN correctly stays RECEIVED (transaction rolled back). Root cause: Map.of() in GoodsReceiptServiceImpl line 260 — audit detail map — throws NPE when req.reason() is null |
| DEF-057 | Medium | Projects | ProjectTaskController | Task created on CANCELLED project - no project status guard | `POST /api/v1/project-tasks/project/uid/{projectUid}` | HTTP 409 or 422, project is cancelled and closed | HTTP 200; task uid=01KVMD1ZKFYT3SN7GJYDCYT0HE created on CANCELLED project - no project status guard in ProjectTaskServiceImpl |

| DEF-059 | Medium | Purchases — PO/Req/Return/RFQ/Quote | PurchaseSettingsController | Update settings with negative poApprovalThresholdAmount bypasses @PositiveOrZero | `PUT /api/v1/purchase-settings` | HTTP 400 validation error (must be >= 0) | HTTP 200; poApprovalThresholdAmount=-500.00 accepted and persisted — @PositiveOrZero on UpdatePurchaseSettingsRequest is silently ignored because PurchaseSettingsController PUT uses @RequestBody without @Valid annotation |
